Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The atoms variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 358 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 688 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the atoms decomposition. By minimality, the element a^358 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 688-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
